\doxysubsection{Detailed Description}
Interface class for RTPlan\+IOD \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Because of its many member variables, an instance of this class requires quite some memory. So be careful when creating automatic variables from this class (the stack size might be exceeded); it is probably better to use the heap space. 
\end{DoxyNote}
